			<script>(function(){
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    // run only on desktop (≥1025px)
    if (window.innerWidth < 1025) return;

    const main = document.querySelector('#menu-item-73 > ul');
    if (!main) return;

    const items = Array.from(main.querySelectorAll('li.menu-item-has-children'));
    const moved = [];

    items.forEach((li) => {
      let childUl = null;
      try { childUl = li.querySelector(':scope > ul'); } catch(e) {}
      if (!childUl) {
        childUl = Array.from(li.children).find(c => c.tagName === 'UL') || null;
      }
      if (!childUl) return;

      if (!li.id) li.id = 'menu-li-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,9);

      childUl.dataset.origin = li.id;
      document.body.appendChild(childUl);
      childUl.classList.add('floating-submenu');

      childUl.style.display = 'none';
      childUl.style.position = 'fixed';
      childUl.style.zIndex = '99999';
      childUl.style.maxHeight = 'none';
      childUl.style.overflow = 'visible';

      moved.push({ li: li, ul: childUl });
    });

    function show(ul, li) {
      const rect = li.getBoundingClientRect();
      ul.style.display = 'block';
      ul.style.visibility = 'hidden';
      requestAnimationFrame(function () {
        const w = ul.offsetWidth;
        const h = ul.offsetHeight;
        let left = rect.right;
        if (left + w > window.innerWidth - 8) left = rect.left - w;
        let top = rect.top;
        if (top + h > window.innerHeight - 8) top = Math.max(8, window.innerHeight - h - 8);
        if (top < 8) top = 8;
        ul.style.left = left + 'px';
        ul.style.top  = top  + 'px';
        ul.style.visibility = 'visible';
      });
    }

    function hide(ul) {
      ul.style.display = 'none';
    }

    moved.forEach(({ li, ul }) => {
      li.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => show(ul, li));
      li.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (!ul.matches(':hover') && !li.matches(':hover')) hide(ul);
        }, 120);
      });
      ul.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => show(ul, li));
      ul.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (!ul.matches(':hover') && !li.matches(':hover')) hide(ul);
        }, 120);
      });
    });

    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => moved.forEach(m => hide(m.ul)), { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => moved.forEach(m => hide(m.ul)));
  });
